Reports what changed upstream since the last sync, ports what applies, records what was deliberately skipped, and refreshes ~/.claude. Use for /p-update, "update pstack", "sync with upstream", or "is pstack out of date". ([negativeblueprint/pstack-claude](https://github.com/negativeblueprint/pstack-claude/tree/HEAD/skills/p-update))
- **p-value-adjustment-methodology**: Use when after running DESeq() to fit negative binomial GLMs and obtaining raw p-values from results(dds), when you need to reduce false positives from multiple testing across thousands of genes while maximizing statistical power. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v1/skills/p-value-adjustment-methodology))
- **p-value-and-confidence-interval-interpretation**: Use when after running gentab_lm_long with mode='lm' to obtain a results table for multiple metabolomic features. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/p-value-and-confidence-interval-interpretation))
- **p-value-and-fold-change-computation**: Use when you have raw metabolomics data organized as a matrix with metabolites as rows and samples as columns, sample group labels (e. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/p-value-and-fold-change-computation))
- **p-value-combination-fisher-method**: Use when when you have tabulated p-values and study sample sizes (N) from multiple independent metabolomics studies measuring the same compounds or metabolites, and you need to compute a single meta-analytic p-value that reflects both statistical significance and study weight. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/p-value-combination-fisher-method))
- **p-value-combination-method-taxonomy**: Use when an analysis requires choosing or cataloguing a method to combine p-values or z-scores (independent or correlated, e.g. GWAS SNPs), or when comparing method coverage across p-value combination packages. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/p-value-combination-method-taxonomy))
- **p-value-combination-weighted-fisher**: Use when individual metabolomics studies report only P-values and fold-changes (no raw data, SD, or variance) and a combined significance per metabolite across studies is needed. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/p-value-combination-weighted-fisher))
- **p-value-comparison-table-compilation**: Use when benchmarking group-level metabolite pathway enrichment tools on a shared metabolite list and you need a side-by-side comparison of each tool's top significant pathway and its enrichment P value. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/p-value-comparison-table-compilation))
- **p-value-computation-and-multiple-testing-correction**: Use when after matched lipid abundances have been grouped by experimental condition and descriptive statistics (mean, SD, median) calculated per group. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/p-value-computation-and-multiple-testing-correction))
- **p-value-cutoff-threshold-filtering**: Use when after differential expression analysis (DEA) has produced p-values and log fold-change values for multiple omics layers (genes, miRNA, proteins, lipids), and you need to select only statistically significant features for pathway enrichment. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/p-value-cutoff-threshold-filtering))
- **p-value-distribution-aggregation**: Use when comparing robustness of pathway ranking methods on metabolomics peak data by repeating an analysis over multiple random feature-removal replicates and summarising each method's p-value distribution as mean and variance. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/p-value-distribution-aggregation))
- **p-value-distribution-normalization**: Use when integrating p-values/scores from multiple genomic assays whose distributions occupy different ranges — e.g. before SMITE scorePval — and you need scores comparable to a reference (expression) without changing within-feature ranking. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/p-value-distribution-normalization))
- **p-value-integration-across-deg-methods**: Use when multiple DEG detection method outputs (per-gene p-value vectors) are available and a machine-learning integration of DEG calls is needed, rather than a simple vote threshold cut-off. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/p-value-integration-across-deg-methods))
- **p-value-integration-combination**: Use when you need robust DEG detection from RNA-seq count matrices and no single DE method can be assumed appropriate, since methods 'behave quite differently depending on the properties of the data'. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/p-value-integration-combination))
- **p-value-interpretation-metabolomics**: Use when after fitting repeated measures ANOVA models to long-format imputed metabolomics data with a grouping variable (e.g., treatment, disease state) and subject-level random effects. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/p-value-interpretation-metabolomics))
- **p-value-multiple-testing-adjustment**: Use when combining two independent differential-expression p-values (sender ligand, receiver receptor) into one LR-pair significance and needing a multiple-testing-adjusted p-value to filter candidate ligand-receptor associations. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/p-value-multiple-testing-adjustment))
- **p-value-multiple-testing-correction**: Use when a pathway overrepresentation analysis produces a table of raw per-pathway p-values from Fisher's exact test (or Fisher's-method combined metabolite/gene p-values) and they must be corrected for multiple testing before ranking or cutoff filtering. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/p-value-multiple-testing-correction))
- **p-value-rank-correlation-analysis**: Use when you need to compare per-gene p-value rankings produced by two versions or variants of a feature-scanning method across multiple datasets, to quantify whether a methodological change (e.g., binary detection vs continuous activity) preserves DEG predictions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/p-value-rank-correlation-analysis))
- **p-value-scatterplot-data-generation**: Use when two DEG-prediction methods have been run on the same scRNA-seq dataset and you need per-gene p-value tables and top-N rank-overlap counts to quantify agreement. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/p-value-scatterplot-data-generation))
- **p-value-significance-verification**: Use when two SVG/DEG detection methods have been run on the same spatial dataset and you must verify and report which features agree or show discrepant ranks and p-values between them. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/p-value-significance-verification))
- **p-value-threshold-based-motif-filtering**: Use when converting cell-line enhancer FASTA sequences (e.g. data/train/NHLF.fasta) into numeric motif features for enhancer prediction, requiring PWM sliding-window scanning with p-value threshold filtering. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/p-value-threshold-based-motif-filtering))
- **p-value-trace-visualization**: Use when you have run progressive permutation of a binary grouping factor over a microbiome feature table and need to visually assess whether the most differential features from the original data retain signal strength (as -log10 p-values) as mixing approaches full permutation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/p-value-trace-visualization))
- **p-value-verification-against-publication-text**: Use when extracting reported model significance values (e.g. OPLS-DA p-values) from a metabolomics paper's text/figures and validating each against its verbatim source span before writing to a results table. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/p-value-verification-against-publication-text))

- **p10**: The Power of Ten: Gerard Holzmann's (NASA/JPL) ten rules for safety-critical code. Use when writing, reviewing, or refactoring code that must be reliable or statically verifiable, or when the user runs /p10 or asks for the Power of Ten rules. - **p1001-can-t-reach-database-server-at-y-d4a56ea505d5c863**: Fixes Prisma error P1001 'Can't reach database server' by diagnosing which part of the connection path is wrong: host, port, pooler mode, or a server that is not actually running. Use when Prisma fails to open any connection at all. Not for authentication failures (P1000) or missing databases (P1003). ([Vectle/skills](https://github.com/Vectle/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/p1001-can-t-reach-database-server-at-y-d4a56ea505d5c863))
- **p1003-database-mydb-does-not-exist-on-03d1db6ed0394f57**: Fixes Prisma error P1003 'Database does not exist on the database server' by creating the missing database and then running migrations against it. ([Vectle/skills](https://github.com/Vectle/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/p1003-database-mydb-does-not-exist-on-03d1db6ed0394f57))
- **p1017-server-has-closed-the-connection-1f4f5f623a3d7bd0**: This skill fixes Prisma error P1017, where the database server closes the connection mid-query.
